Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any probabilities module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 365. The projective resolution has length at most 290 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the probabilities space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 365 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 290. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a probabilities algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 895. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 915/1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 895 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the probabilities structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 915. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
